{
  "text": "## 审查报告 — worker_code_phase2_dryrun_verify + Registry Sync 后续\n\n**结论**: WARNING\n\n### Registry Sync 状态\n\n- [severity: info] **Registry 已同步**。grammar_only→RUNNING（含 crash 误报诊断），unconstrained→RUNNING，phase2_s2_pilot50 已创建。crash 根因确认为 cron 追踪 stale PID 123425 的误报，实际进程从未崩溃。总实验数 8→9。\n\n### phase2_s2_pilot50 结果核实\n\n- [severity: warning] **relation_f1=0.0 是真问题，非评估 bug**。手动核实了评估代码（violation_checker.py:236）：`rel_key` 匹配条件为 `(head_text, tail_text, relation_type)`，不含 entity type——比许多 RE 评估更宽松。在这个宽松条件下仍然 0/25 TP，说明 S2 两通道架构存在系统性问题。\n\n  手动抽查前 3 个样本发现两类 root cause：\n  1. **Entity 边界不一致**：模型提取 \"Multi-lingual Evaluation Task\" 但 gold 是 \"Multi-lingual Evaluation Task -LRB- MET -RRB-\"，导致 head/tail text 永远不匹配\n  2. **Relation type 错误**：即使 head/tail text 匹配（如 morphological analysis → Japanese text processing），模型预测 Part-of 但 gold 是 Used-for\n\n  对比 Phase 0 unconstrained 的 relation_f1=0.0823：S2 的两通道架构反而**劣于无约束 baseline**。这对论文 story 是重大风险——约束不能以牺牲全部 relation quality 为代价。\n\n- [severity: info] **D/R violation=0%, RV violation=0%**——约束执行层面运作正确。问题不在约束本身，而在 S2 的 Pass 1→Pass 2 error propagation。\n\n- [severity: info] Registry 记录 phase2_s2_pilot50 的 spec.gpu 为 [\"cuda:0\"]，但该进程实际在物理 GPU-1 上执行（CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1）。进程已完成，不影响结果，但 spec 不准确。\n\n### 建议\n\n1. S2 relation_f1 root cause 调查已在进行中（registry 提及 Worker analysis:s2_relation_f1_rootcause），建议优先级 P0\n2. **论文 framing 风险**：如果 S2 不能恢复 relation F1，需要重新评估是否保留 S2 作为核心方法。S1（single-pass grammar+semantic）可能是更可行的路径——等 phase2_scierc_full_s1 结果\n3. Entity 边界匹配问题可能是所有 constrained 模式的共性问题（不仅限于 S2），建议在 grammar_only 完成后检查其 relation_f1",

```text
Now I have the Phase 2 unconstrained results. Let me cross-validate against Phase 0:

**Phase 2 unconstrained vs Phase 0 unconstrained:**

| Metric | Phase 0 | Phase 2 unconstrained |
|--------|---------|----------------------|
| total_samples | 551 | 551 |
| total_triples | 1724 | 1724 |
| total_entities | 2474 | 2474 |
| json_parse_rate | 1.0 | 1.0 |
| entity_type_violation_rate | 0.004 | 0.004 |


| relation_vocab_violation_rate | 0.0139 | 0.0139 |
| domain_range_violation_rate | 0.1537 | 0.1537 |
| cross_field_consistency_violation_rate | 0.0008 | 0.0008 |
| entity_f1 | 0.3101 | 0.3101 |
| relation_f1 | 0.0823 | 0.0823 |

These metrics are completely identical across both phases—every single value matches perfectly, which is surprising and worth verifying.

Looking at the detailed metrics from Phase 0 and Phase 2 unconstrained, they're showing the same entity F1 of 0.3101, relation F1 of 0.0823, and identical violation rates. This makes sense since both use the same Qwen3-8B model, same data, and same seed, with the only difference being that Phase 2 runs through a different script with the unconstrained mode flag, which shouldn't actually apply any constraints anyway.

When I examine the precision and recall breakdown for Phase 2 unconstrained versus the grammar_only variant, they're nearly identical—both around 0.26 precision and 0.38 recall with F1 scores differing only in the third decimal place. This confirms that the code path change from run_extraction.py to run_constrained_extraction.py with mode=unconstrained produces deterministic results matching the original baseline, validating that the unconstrained mode truly applies no constraints. Looking at the per-relation breakdown, Phase 0 and Phase 2 unconstrained show identical results across all relation types—Used-for, Feature-of, Evaluate-for, Hyponym-of, Part-of, Compare, and Conjunction all have the same violation counts and rates, suggesting the grammar constraints didn't meaningfully change the distribution of errors by relation type. The Phase 2 unconstrained baseline also captures relation violation examples that grammar_only would filter out, confirming the deterministic behavior with our seed and decoding strategy. I should post the review now, though I'll note that these metrics are still being analyzed and haven't been registered yet.
```
